```ts
const MERGE_LINE = /^- \[(J[0-9]+#[0-9]+)\] [^：]*：(.+)$/;
const MATCH_LINE = /^- \[(R[0-9]+)\] [^：]*：(.+)$/;

function requirementPlanByText(text: string): DemoRequirementPlan | null {
  for (const plan of DEMO_REQUIREMENT_PLANS) if (plan.text === text) return plan;
  return null;
}

/** jd-extract：只把引文真的出现在这份 JD 里的要求吐出去（不假装抽到了 JD 里没有的东西） */
function jdExtractText(prompt: string): string {
  const requirements = [];
  for (const plan of DEMO_REQUIREMENT_PLANS) {
    if (prompt.indexOf(plan.quote) < 0) continue;
    requirements.push({ text: plan.text, quote: plan.quote, kind: plan.kind, keywords: plan.keywords });
  }
  return JSON.stringify({ requirements });
}
```

```ts
/** jd-merge：演示只有一份 JD，没有可并的——如实一条一组，不为了好看硬凑成一组 */
function jdMergeText(prompt: string): string {
  const groups = [];
  for (const line of prompt.split(NL)) {
    const hit = MERGE_LINE.exec(line.trim());
    if (hit === null) continue;
    const text = hit[2].trim();
    const plan = requirementPlanByText(text);
    groups.push({
      text,
      kind: plan === null ? 'must' : plan.kind,
      members: [hit[1]],
      keywords: plan === null ? [] : plan.keywords,
    });
  }
  return JSON.stringify({ groups });
}
```

Functions text:

```
/** 提示词里每条已抽要求的渲染形状：'- [J1#3] 硬性要求：<text>' 换行后跟 '  原文：<quote>' */
const MERGE_LINE = /^- \[(J[0-9]+#[0-9]+)\] ([^：]*)：(.+)$/;
/** 提示词里每条要求的渲染形状：'- [R1] 硬性要求：<text>' */
const MATCH_LINE = /^- \[(R[0-9]+)\] ([^：]*)：(.+)$/;

function requirementPlanByText(text: string): DemoRequirementPlan | null {
  for (const plan of DEMO_REQUIREMENT_PLANS) if (plan.text === text) return plan;
  return null;
}

function kindOfLabel(label: string): RequirementKind {
  const keys = Object.keys(REQUIREMENT_KIND_LABEL) as RequirementKind[];
  for (const key of keys) if (REQUIREMENT_KIND_LABEL[key] === label) return key;
  return 'must';
}

/** jd-extract：只吐引文真出现在这份 JD 里的要求，不假装抽到了 JD 里没有的东西 */
function jdExtractText(prompt: string): string {
  const requirements: DemoRequirementDraft[] = [];
  for (const plan of DEMO_REQUIREMENT_PLANS) {
    if (prompt.indexOf(plan.quote) < 0) continue;
    requirements.push({ text: plan.text, quote: plan.quote, kind: plan.kind, keywords: plan.keywords });
  }
  return JSON.stringify({ requirements });
}

/**
 * jd-merge：演示只有一份 JD，没有可并的——如实一条一组。
 * 硬凑成一组虽然在演示里更好看，但那是在伪造"跨份归并"这个动作。
 */
function jdMergeText(prompt: string): string {
  const groups: { text: string; kind: RequirementKind; members: string[]; keywords: string[] }[] = [];
  for (const raw of prompt.split(NL)) {
    const line = raw.trim();
    const hit = MERGE_LINE.exec(line);
    if (hit === null) continue;
    const text = (hit[3] ?? '').trim();
    const plan = requirementPlanByText(text);
    groups.push({
      text,
      kind: kindOfLabel(hit[2] ?? ''),
      members: [hit[1] ?? ''],
      keywords: plan === null ? [] : plan.keywords,
    });
  }
  return JSON.stringify({ groups });
}

/** jd-match：逐条要求对照，结果写死在 DEMO_REQUIREMENT_PLANS 里，不临场编 */
function jdMatchText(prompt: string): string {
  const claimIds = new Set<string>();
  const claimHit = /\[(C[0-9]+)\]/g;
  let found = claimHit.exec(prompt);
  while (found !== null) {
    claimIds.add(found[1] ?? '');
    found = claimHit.exec(prompt);
  }
  const matches: { requirementId: string; claimId: string | null; status: MatchStatus; reason: string }[] = [];
  for (const raw of prompt.split(NL)) {
    const line = raw.trim();
    const hit = MATCH_LINE.exec(line);
    if (hit === null) continue;
    const requirementId = hit[1] ?? '';
    const plan = requirementPlanByText((hit[3] ?? '').trim());
    if (plan === null) {
      matches.push({ requirementId, claimId: null, status: 'missing', reason: '演示脚本没有为这条要求准备对照结果，按简历里没写处理。' });
      continue;
    }
    const wanted = plan.match.claimId;
    if (wanted === null || !claimIds.has(wanted)) {
      matches.push({ requirementId, claimId: null, status: 'missing', reason: plan.match.reason });
      continue;
    }
    matches.push({ requirementId, claimId: wanted, status: plan.match.status, reason: plan.match.reason });
  }
  return JSON.stringify({ matches });
}
```

The rendered line is `- [R1] 硬性要求：<text>`; text is the first `：`-separated remainder. `(.+)$` greedy takes the rest fine. But if text contains `：`, `(.+)$` handles it because `[^：]*` is non-greedy-ish: regex engine will try the first `：`. Yes.

```ts
/** JD 标题的兜底：用户没填标题时，取正文第一行非空文本——JD 通常第一行就是岗位名 */
function firstLineOf(text: string): string {
  for (const line of text.split(String.fromCharCode(10))) {
    const trimmed = line.trim();
    if (trimmed !== '') return trimmed;
  }
  return '';
}
```

The whole run body is inside `try { ... } catch`. A throwing jd-extract means the entire run fails (recorded as error). That's a design question: "坏一份 JD 不该毁一场" — but a *thrown* client error (network) does fail the whole run via extract's Promise.all. The comment says single-JD *parse* failure only skips that JD. <details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

Confirmed: `probe.requirementId` / `probe.jdAnchor` are **not persisted**. This is a real gap for P8: the double anchor is a core evidence of the feature ("追问带第二个锚点"), and reopening a historical session (or the report, which reads from the DB via SessionView) loses it.

So P8 needs schema columns on probe: `requirement_id TEXT`, `jd_quote TEXT`, `jd_start INTEGER`, `jd_end INTEGER`, `jd_exact INTEGER`. Since v6 migration is already written (and SCHEMA_VERSION=6 now), adding columns to the probe table means:
- If the DB is fresh (user_version 0), DDL creates the new probe table (with columns) — fine.
- If a DB is already at v6 (e.g. my own local dev DB at .tmp or the user's), we need a v7 migration. Since v6 hasn't been released/committed yet (work is uncommitted), I could just extend v6's migration to also add the probe columns. But v6's migrateV6 is "only adds new tables". Adding probe columns requires rebuild of probe table → migrateV6 would need to rebuild probe. That's fine as long as no released database is at v6. Since P8 is uncommitted and v6 was introduced in this same uncommitted work, extending migrateV6 is legitimate. But careful: any DB that already ran v6 (my `npm run seed:demo` DB? that uses fixtures; the dev DB .tmp/... may be at v6) would then be at v6 but without the columns → mismatch. Safer: bump to v7 with migrateV7 that rebuilds probe with the new columns, and keep migrateV6 as-is. That's the honest, forward-only approach and matches the AGENTS.md "旧的不要改，只能新增" spirit for ADRs (though migrations are code). Also the pattern: PROBE_COLUMNS constant used for migration copying. I need a new column list for v6→v7 rebuild.

Actually simpler: since migrateV6 now = create the 3 JD tables; if I add columns to `probeTableSql`, then:
- Fresh DB: DDL includes new columns. ✓
- DB at v5: migrateV6 runs with new probeTableSql? No — migrateV6 only creates JD tables; probe table would remain old (no new columns) but version becomes 6 → broken.
So I must add a rebuild step. Options:
(a) Add probe rebuild into migrateV6 (rename probe → probe_v5, create new probe, copy old columns, drop old) and append new columns as NULL. Then fresh and v5→v6 both correct. But a DB already at v6 (from my earlier test runs — do any exist? tests use :memory:; dev DBs: `.tmp/live-full/live.db`, `.tmp/e2e/db/e2e.db`, maybe `data/crossexam.db`?) would be stale. Those are throwaway test DBs, but the *user's* DB (if they ran the app during P7) would be at v5 → 升到 v6 works. Actually wait: has any DB been migrated to v6 in a real place? SCHEMA_VERSION=6 was introduced in this uncommitted P8 work; if I ran `npm run seed:demo` it doesn't open a user DB (fixtures only). e2e/live test DBs are created fresh each run. But to be safe and honest, bump SCHEMA_VERSION=7 with migrateV7 rebuilding probe. Then:
- fresh → DDL (new probe cols) ✓
- v5 → v6 (JD tables) → v7 (probe rebuild) ✓
- v6 (stale, from my own runs) → v7 ✓

"列从来没变过" is now false. New:
```
/**
 * probe 表的建表语句。这里用一个参数区分 v2 与 v3 的形态（唯一约束在 v3 被去掉），
 * 避免把整张表抄两遍。v7 给这张表补了「第二条锚」五列（requirement_id / jd_*）——
 * 那是加列不是换约束，所以参数只需要区分约束这一个差异。
 */
```

Then migrateV7 after migrateV6:

```
/**
 * v6 → v7：追问表补上「第二条锚」（岗位要求原文）。
 *
 * 双锚点是这个功能的证据本身：报告与历史会话都要能回答「这条追问是照着哪条岗位要求问的」。
 * 只把它留在事件流里不够——重开一场历史会话读的是业务表，事件流是给重连与复现用的，
 * 两处都得有。（这是自审时抓到的：写测试要断言双锚点时才发现它根本没落库。）
 * 老库里的追问一律按 jd_* 全 NULL 处理，语义正好是「这条追问只锚了简历」。
 */
const PROBE_COLUMNS_V6 =
  'id, session_id, claim_id, interviewer_id, round, question, rationale, parent_probe_id, quote, start, end, exact, answer_text, answer_mode, answer_cites_json, verdict, created_at';

function migrateV7(db: Db): void {
  rebuild(
    db,
    () => {
      db.exec('DROP INDEX IF EXISTS probe_by_claim');
      db.exec('ALTER TABLE probe RENAME TO probe_v6');
      db.exec(probeTableSql(false));
      db.exec(PROBE_INDEX);
      db.exec('INSERT INTO probe (' + PROBE_COLUMNS_V6 + ') SELECT ' + PROBE_COLUMNS_V6 + ' FROM probe_v6');
      db.exec('DROP TABLE probe_v6');
    },
    7,
  );
}
```
And MIGRATIONS: add `7: migrateV7,`.

Let me apply.

All green. Now write the JD server test file `packages/server/test/jd.test.ts`.

Design (final):

```ts
/**
 * 岗位要求（多 JD 驱动）的端到端测试。
 *
 * 这层要守住四件事，每一件都能在报告里被指出来：
 *   1. 多份 JD 各自抽要求、跨份合并成一条，编号与来源由代码算，不是模型说了算；
 *   2. 对照结果每条要求都有一条，模型漏的、指错主张的都由代码补成「简历里没写」；
 *   3. 追问的第二个锚点（岗位要求原文）锚不回 JD 时退回普通追问，并写明为什么；
 *   4. 双锚点要能落库读回来——重开一场历史会话时它不能只剩一个锚。
 */
```

JD texts: JD_A and JD_B.

JD_A (岗位 A):
```
高级后端工程师（Node.js）

岗位职责：
1. 负责交易与订单核心链路的架构设计与性能优化；

任职要求：
1. 本科及以上学历，5 年以上后端研发经验；
2. 精通 Node.js / TypeScript，熟悉 PostgreSQL、Redis。
```
Check the quote '5 年以上后端研发经验' appears verbatim: line '1. 本科及以上学历，5 年以上后端研发经验；' yes.
'精通 Node.js / TypeScript，熟悉 PostgreSQL、Redis' appears verbatim: line '2. 精通 Node.js / TypeScript，熟悉 PostgreSQL、Redis。' yes (without trailing 。).

Length: ~110 chars > 40. ok.

JD_B (岗位 B, 第一行作标题):
```
数据平台后端工程师

任职要求：
1. 5 年以上后端研发经验，有大规模系统经验；
2. 熟悉 Kafka 与 Flink。
```
contains '5 年以上后端研发经验' ✓ (note JD_A has the same text but preceded by '，5 年...' consistent).

The merged R1 text: which text wins? mergeExtracted picks... group text from the model: I supply '5 年以上后端研发经验'? But then it must anchor — requirement anchors come from member quotes, so fine. I'll set merged group text = '5 年以上后端研发经验（5 年及以上）'? Keep it simple: text = '5 年以上后端研发经验'. R2 text = '精通 Node.js / TypeScript，熟悉 PostgreSQL、Redis'.

Now requirement ids after merge: R1 = group1 (first appearance J1#1), R2 = group2. Requirement jdId for R1: 代表句出自哪份 JD（合并后取最长引文那一份）. Member quotes:
- J1#1 quote '5 年以上后端研发经验' (len 11 rune-ish; JS length: '5 年以上后端研发经验' = 11 chars: 5, space, 年,以,上,后,端,研,发,经,验 = 11)
- J2#1 quote '5 年以上后端研发经验' = same 11.
Tie → let me check mergeExtracted's tie-break: "代表锚点取引文最长的". If tie, whichever comes first in iteration → J1. So jdId = 'J1', sources = ['J1','J2'].

Order of items from extract: extractRequirements iterates jds in order J1, J2 and pushes items → [J1#1, J1#2(?), ...]. Wait J1 has 3 drafts: accepted #1 (#1 must 年限), #2 (must Node.js), #3 rejected (plus 带团队). So items = [J1#1, J1#2, J2#1]. Merge groups: group1 members [J1#1, J2#1], group2 members [J1#2]. Orphan none. built order by first appearance → R1 = group1, R2 = group2. 

Note in merge, does the built requirement keep `text` from the model group text or from a member? I said "谁并进了谁、编号怎么排全部由代码算" — text likely from group.text (model), validated 4-200 chars. I'll set group text to exactly the requirement sentence.

Careful about the rejected draft's key numbering: groundExtracted assigns `key` = requirementKey(jd.id, index+1) presumably over drafts in order; the rejected one is index 3 → J1#3. It's in `rejected`, not in items. Fine.

Now `req.channel` for jd-extract is 'jd-extract:J1'/'J2'. I'll switch on `req.channel`.

Now scripted handler factory:

```ts
interface ScriptOptions {
  /** jd-match 返回什么；默认两条都给 */
  match?: string;
  /** 第二轮追问的引文；默认给一个锚不回 JD 的引文，用来验证退回普通追问 */
  secondQuote?: string;
  /** 记下每一次调用，便于断言提示词里真的摆了岗位要求 */

}
```

- round 1: `{claimId:'C1', question:'这个是 5 年以上还是 3 年以上？', quote: QUOTE, rationale:'岗位要求写了年限，简历没写', requirementId:'R1', jdQuote: '5 年以上后端研发经验'}`

I'll add a v6→v7 test in store.test.ts following the v2 pattern: build a v6-shaped DB (with JD tables), insert a probe row (v6 columns, 17 incl. answer_cites_json), migrate → version 7, probe survives, jdAnchor null; then insert a probe *with* jdAnchor and read it back (proving the new columns work).

That last part is the real regression guard for the persistence gap.

How to construct the legacy DB? Simplest: create a full v6 DB by... hmm, DDL is now v7. I could use `openDatabase({skipMigrate:true})` and exec the v6 DDL manually — that requires copying lots of SQL. Alternative: cheat by building via current DDL then dropping the new columns? Not possible.

Simpler approach: build a *minimal but sufficient* v6 DB: session, claim, probe (v6 shape with FK), event, llm_call, plus the three JD tables (needed because migrateV7 doesn't touch them... actually migrateV6 only creates them; when migrating from v6 to v7, they must exist — my legacy DB must already have them, but if missing, migrateV7 doesn't need them; migrate() steps from 6 → 7 only runs migrateV7. So JD tables aren't needed at all for this test! I just need session/claim/probe/event/llm_call as in the v2 test, plus `PRAGMA user_version = 6`.

But careful: `rebuild` wraps in a transaction; probe table rebuild requires the index name. Fine.

So: copy the v2 test's minimal DDL but give probe the v6 shape (with answer_cites_json, no UNIQUE constraint — v3 shape). Insert session/claim/probe rows with 17 values. migrate → SCHEMA_VERSION (7). Assert probes[0].text/answer survive and requirementId/jdAnchor are null. Then insertProbe a probe with jdAnchor and assert round-trip.

```ts
    const legacy = openDatabase({ path: ':memory:', skipMigrate: true });

          ' mode TEXT NOT NULL, job_title TEXT NOT NULL, jd_text TEXT NOT NULL, resume_text TEXT NOT NULL,' +
          ' resume_hash TEXT NOT NULL, status TEXT NOT NULL, budget_json TEXT NOT NULL,' +

      legacy.exec(

          ' ordinal INTEGER NOT NULL, text TEXT NOT NULL, category TEXT NOT NULL, weight REAL NOT NULL,' +
          ' quote TEXT NOT NULL, start INTEGER NOT NULL, end INTEGER NOT NULL, exact INTEGER NOT NULL,' +
          ' status TEXT NOT NULL, verdict TEXT, advice_json TEXT,' +
          ' PRIMARY KEY (session_id, id), UNIQUE (session_id, ordinal))',
      );
      legacy.exec('CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS claim_by_session ON claim(session_id, ordinal)');

      legacy.exec('CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS probe_by_claim ON probe(session_id, claim_id, round)');
      legacy.exec('CREATE TABLE event (...)');
      legacy.exec('CREATE TABLE llm_call (...)');
      legacy.exec('PRAGMA user_version = 6');
      ... insert session, claim, probe (17 values incl answer_cites_json null)
      expect(migrate(legacy)).toBe(SCHEMA_VERSION);
      const probes = listProbes(legacy, 'sess-v6');
      expect(probes).toHaveLength(1);
      expect(probes[0]?.question).toBe('老问题？');

      const back = listProbes(legacy, 'sess-v6').find(function (p) { return p.id === 'P2'; });
      expect(back?.requirementId).toBe('R1');
      expect(back?.jdAnchor?.quote).toBe('5 年以上后端研发经验');
      expect(back?.jdAnchor?.exact).toBe(true);
    } finally {
```

Now create `packages/server/test/jd.test.ts`. Avoid double quotes in content entirely by using JSON.stringify at runtime and single quotes.

Structure:

```ts
/**
 * 岗位要求（多 JD 驱动）的端到端测试。
 * ...
 */

import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { createApp } from '../src/http/app.ts';
import { closeDatabase, migrate, openDatabase } from '../src/store/index.ts';
import type { Db } from '../src/store/index.ts';
import type { Probe, Requirement, RequirementMatch } from '@crossexam/shared';
import { createScriptedClient } from './scripted.ts';
import type { ScriptedClient } from './scripted.ts';

const NL = String.fromCharCode(10);

const JD_A = [...]
const JD_B = [...]

const RESUME = ['项目经历','主导重构订单系统，首屏从 2.4s 降到 0.8s，日订单量 30 万。','熟悉 TypeScript 与 Node.js。'].join(NL);
const QUOTE = '首屏从 2.4s 降到 0.8s';

const EXTRACT = JSON.stringify({ claims: [{ text: '主导订单系统重构，把首屏从 2.4s 降到 0.8s', quote: QUOTE, category: 'achievement', weight: 0.9 }] });

const R1_TEXT = '5 年以上后端研发经验';
const R2_TEXT = '精通 Node.js / TypeScript，熟悉 PostgreSQL、Redis';

const JD_EXTRACT_A = JSON.stringify({ requirements: [
  { text: R1_TEXT, quote: R1_TEXT, kind: 'must', keywords: ['年限'] },
  { text: R2_TEXT, quote: R2_TEXT, kind: 'must', keywords: ['Node.js', 'Redis'] },
  // 这条故意锚不到 JD 原文：验证「锚不回的要求宁可丢掉」这条口径
  { text: '有带团队的经验', quote: '有带团队的经验', kind: 'plus', keywords: [] },
]});

const JD_EXTRACT_B = JSON.stringify({ requirements: [
  { text: R1_TEXT, quote: R1_TEXT, kind: 'must', keywords: ['年限'] },
]});

const JD_MERGE = JSON.stringify({ groups: [
  { text: R1_TEXT, kind: 'must', members: ['J1#1', 'J2#1'], keywords: ['年限'] },
  { text: R2_TEXT, kind: 'must', members: ['J1#2'], keywords: ['Node.js'] },
]});

const JD_MATCH = JSON.stringify({ matches: [
  { requirementId: 'R1', claimId: 'C1', status: 'weak', reason: '简历写了后端经验，但没写年限' },
  { requirementId: 'R2', claimId: null, status: 'missing', reason: '简历没有提数据库与缓存' },
]});

const ANSWER = JSON.stringify({ answered: true, text: '这是本地 Chrome 冷启动测的', citedQuotes: [QUOTE] });
const VERDICT = JSON.stringify({ kind: 'solid', confidence: 0.8, rationale: '有具体数字也有测量口径', evidenceQuotes: [QUOTE] });
const NO_SUGGEST = JSON.stringify({ suggestions: [] });

function probeJson(question: string, extra: Record<string, unknown>): string {
  return JSON.stringify({ probes: [{ claimId: 'C1', question, quote: QUOTE, rationale: '…', ...extra }] });
}

/** 两轮追问：第一轮带上岗位要求原文（双锚点），第二轮故意给一个锚不回 JD 的引文 */
function scripted(options: { match?: string } = {}): ScriptedClient {
  let probeCalls = 0;
  return createScriptedClient((req) => {
    if (req.purpose === 'extract') return EXTRACT;
    if (req.purpose === 'jd-extract') return req.channel === 'jd-extract:J1' ? JD_EXTRACT_A : JD_EXTRACT_B;
    if (req.purpose === 'jd-merge') return JD_MERGE;
    if (req.purpose === 'jd-match') return options.match ?? JD_MATCH;
    if (req.purpose === 'probe') {
      probeCalls += 1;
      if (probeCalls === 1) return probeJson('岗位写 5 年以上，你简历里没写年限，怎么证明？', { requirementId: 'R1', jdQuote: R1_TEXT });
      if (probeCalls === 2) return probeJson('PostgreSQL 和 Redis 你分别用在哪？', { requirementId: 'R2', jdQuote: '这句话不在 JD 原文里' });
      return JSON.stringify({ probes: [] });
    }
    if (req.purpose === 'answer') return ANSWER;
    if (req.purpose === 'verdict') return VERDICT;
    if (req.purpose === 'suggest') return NO_SUGGEST;
    return new Error('脚本化模型没有为 purpose=' + req.purpose + ' 准备返回值');
  });
}
```

```ts
/**
 * 岗位要求（多 JD 驱动）的端到端测试。
 *
 * 这一层守住四件事，每一件都能被指到具体产物上：

 *   4. 第二处锚必须落库：重开一场历史会话时，它不能只剩下简历那一处。
 */
```

Types for the view — define a local interface `SessionViewLike` with the fields used. Keep it lean but typed to avoid `any`:

```ts
interface SessionViewLike {
  session: { resumeText: string; jdText: string };
  notices: string[];
  probes: Probe[];
  jds: { id: string; ordinal: number; title: string; text: string }[];
  requirements: Requirement[];
  requirementMatches: RequirementMatch[];
  requirementRejected: { key: string; text: string; reason: string }[];
  requirementSummary: { hit: number; weak: number; missing: number; total: number };
}
```

Helper:
```ts
async function viewOf(t: Rig, id: string): Promise<SessionViewLike> {
  const res = await t.app.request('/api/sessions/' + id);
  expect(res.status).toBe(200);
  return (await res.json()) as SessionViewLike;
}
```

Helper createSession, newJdSession:
```ts
async function jdSession(t: Rig, jds: unknown[]): Promise<{ id: string; jds: { id: string; title: string }[] }> {
  const res = await createSession(t, { resumeText: RESUME, jobTitle: '后端工程师', jds });
  expect(res.status).toBe(201);
  return (await res.json()) as { id: string; jds: { id: string; title: string }[] };
}
```

run helper:
```ts
async function run(t: Rig, id: string): Promise<void> {
  const res = await t.app.request('/api/sessions/' + id + '/run?stream=0&panel=tech', { method: 'POST' });
  expect(res.status).toBe(200);
}
```

JdPane.tsx:

```tsx
/**
 * 岗位要求原文栏。
 *
 * 与「简历原文」栏并排：左边是候选人写的话，右边是岗位要求写的话——
 * 质询的每一次追问，锚点不是落在左边就是落在右边，一个都不能少。
 * 这里只负责渲染与高亮，切分逻辑复用 highlight.ts 的同一套（与简历栏一致）。
 */
import { useRef } from 'react';
import { Empty, Pane } from './Bits.tsx';
import { buildSegments, describeRange } from '../highlight.ts';
import type { Anchor, JobDescription } from '../api.ts';

export function JdPane(props: {
  jds: JobDescription[];
  activeId: string | null;
  onPick: (id: string) => void;
  focus: Anchor | null;
  hits: Anchor[];
  className?: string;
}) {
  const focusRef = useRef<HTMLElement | null>(null);
  const focusKey = props.focus === null ? '' : String(props.focus.start) + '-' + String(props.focus.end);
  const active = props.jds.find((jd) => jd.id === props.activeId) ?? props.jds[0] ?? null;

  if (active === null) {
    return (
      <Pane title="岗位要求原文" dataShot="jd" className={props.className} subtitle="还没有上传 JD">
        <Empty
          title="还没有上传岗位要求"
          hint="左边可以贴 1–8 份 JD。没有 JD 也能跑：面试官会按各自的经验追问，只是不会有「岗位要求对照」这一栏。"
        />
      </Pane>
    );
  }

  const segments = buildSegments(active.text, props.focus, props.hits);
  return (
    <Pane
      title="岗位要求原文"
      dataShot="jd"
      className={props.className}
      subtitle={active.title + ' · ' + String(active.text.length) + ' 字' + (props.jds.length > 1 ? ' · 共 ' + String(props.jds.length) + ' 份' : '')}
      actions={ props.jds.length > 1 ? (<div className="flex items-center gap-1">...</div>) : undefined }
      bodyClassName="p-0"
    >
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 p-3">
        ... segments
      </div>
    </Pane>
  );
}
```

```ts
}
```
